30 /**
31 * @brief Interface to the BLAS/cuBLAS `%gemv` routine.
32 *
33 * @details This function performs the matrix-vector operation
34 * \f[
35 * \mathbf{y} \leftarrow \alpha \mathbf{A} \mathbf{x} + \beta \mathbf{y} \; ,
36 * \f]
37 * where \f$ \alpha \f$ and \f$ \beta \f$ are scalars, \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is an \f$ m \times n \f$ matrix and \f$
38 * \mathbf{x} \f$ and \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ are vectors of sizes \f$ n \f$ and \f$ m \f$, respectively.
39 *
40 * If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, the cuBLAS implementation is used.
41 *
42 * @note \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is allowed to be a lazy conjugate expression (see nda::blas_lapack::is_conj_array_expr),
43 * in which case it is required to be in nda::C_layout.
44 *
45 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayOrConj<2> type.
46 * @tparam X nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<A, 1> type.
47 * @tparam Y nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<A, 1> type.
48 * @param alpha Input scalar \f$ \alpha \f$.
49 * @param a Input matrix \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ of size \f$ m \times n \f$.
50 * @param x Input vector \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ of size \f$ n \f$.
51 * @param beta Input scalar \f$ \beta \f$.
52 * @param y Input/Output vector \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ of size \f$ m \f$.
53 */
54 template <BlasArrayOrConj<2> A, BlasArrayFor<A, 1> X, BlasArrayFor<A, 1> Y>
55 129 void gemv(get_value_t<A> alpha, A const &a, X const &x, get_value_t<A> beta, Y &&y) { // NOLINT (temporary views are allowed here)
56 // get the underlying matrix in case it is given as a conjugate expression
57 129 auto &mat = get_array(a);
58
59 // check the dimensions of the input/output arrays/views
60 129 auto [m, n] = mat.shape();
61
